Fix what's there before you rewrite. Say what's actually wrong instead of being polite. Ask questions instead of giving answers. Triggers on: think deeper, review, challenge, argue, question assumptions, devil's advocate, gut feeling, something feels off, tech debt, rewrite vs repair, ship it, root cause, why. - **kmeans-silhouette-cluster-selection**: Use when you have a coefficient matrix of HVGs from CPPLS regression on spatial transcriptomics data and need to group genes with similar neighboring-cell-type regulation patterns prior to building a MIMO communication graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmeans-silhouette-cluster-selection))
- **kmeans-spatial-segmentation**: Use when segmenting imzML mass spectrometry imaging pixels into spatially coherent regions via k-means, and when Silhouette scores must rank clusterings or compare hyperparameter search strategies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kmeans-spatial-segmentation))
- **kmeans-subcluster-family-clustering**: Use when a large set of predicted sub-clusters (e.g. 108,085 PRESTO-STAT sub-clusters from 60,028 antiSMASH-DB BGCs) must be organised into related families and higher-level clans. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kmeans-subcluster-family-clustering))
- **kmeans-subgroup-count-reconciliation**: Use when a paper reports k-means-derived subgroup counts (e.g., phosphoAKT1-high/MYC-high 6/60) that must be re-tabulated into 2x2/2x4 contingency tables and tested against a clinical covariate such as Gleason grade. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kmeans-subgroup-count-reconciliation))
- **kmeans-trajectory-clustering**: Use when a normalized feature-by-donor abundance matrix (peaks or genes) has samples ordered by age and you need to detect and classify features showing genuine monotonic or non-monotonic temporal trends over adult lifespan. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kmeans-trajectory-clustering))
- **kmeans-voronoi-inverted-index-construction**: Use when a sparse pairwise cosine-distance matrix over many MS/MS spectra within a precursor mass tolerance must be computed efficiently, and exhaustive pairwise comparison (e.g. 1908 comparisons/spectrum at 20 ppm) is infeasible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/kmeans-voronoi-inverted-index-construction))
- **kmer-abundance-based-repeat-filtering**: Use when you need to derive sparse, low-repetitiveness seed sequences (e.g. 270 bp or 285 bp) from merged short reads for a long-read seed-based assembler like TULIP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-abundance-based-repeat-filtering))
- **kmer-abundance-distance-computation**: Use when you need all-vs-all pairwise distance matrices between many metagenomic (or viromic) FASTA/FASTQ samples based on k-mer composition and abundance, especially when datasets are too large for sketch-based or normalization-dependent tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-abundance-distance-computation))
- **kmer-abundance-overestimation-scoring**: Use when comparing abundance-reporting AMQ data structures (e.g. fimpera-wrapped vs raw cBF) on how overestimated k-mer abundances trade off against filter size in bits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-abundance-overestimation-scoring))
- **kmer-abundance-query-evaluation**: Use when you have reported k-mer abundances from one or more counting AMQ structures (e.g., counting Bloom filters, fimpera indices) plus ground-truth abundances and need to quantify abundance correctness on true-positive calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-abundance-query-evaluation))
- **kmer-abundance-querying**: Use when abundances of indexed k-mers must be queried from large (e.g. metagenomic/TARA-scale) sequence datasets with low false positive rates and no memory overhead. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-abundance-querying))
- **kmer-abundance-weighting-scheme-selection**: Use when preparing k-mer frequency vectors for Libra all-vs-all metagenome scoring and you must decide whether raw abundance, log-scaled abundance, or presence/absence reflects the biology you want the distance metric to capture. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-abundance-weighting-scheme-selection))
- **kmer-anchor-candidate-filtering**: Use when assembling long erroneous 3GS reads (PacBio/ONT) against short-read contigs and you need to find overlapping long-read pairs without all-vs-all base-level alignment, e.g. tuning DBG2OLC's AdaptiveTh, KmerCovTh, MinOverlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-anchor-candidate-filtering))
- **kmer-annotation-matrix-assembly**: Use when you have filtered peak counts from ATAC or DNase-seq data (with GC bias correction and sample/peak filtering applied) and want to annotate peaks by k-mer content rather than known transcription factor motifs—particularly when comparing how k-mer size affects the magnitude of chromatin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/kmer-annotation-matrix-assembly))
- **kmer-based-assembly-accuracy-quantification**: Use when you need to assess whether a polishing tool improved or degraded a HiFi/T2T assembly, by measuring QV and counting changed K-mers and potential overcorrection K-mers against short-read k-mer datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-assembly-accuracy-quantification))
- **kmer-based-correction-evaluation**: Use when comparing hybrid error-correction outputs (e.g., HECIL, LSC, PacBioToCA, LoRDEC, proovread, CoLoRMap) on PacBio long reads and you need a k-mer-based quality metric or a stopping rule for iterative correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-correction-evaluation))
- **kmer-based-intron-exon-boundary-detection**: Use when you have an assembled transcriptome (FASTA) and raw WGS reads or a prebuilt abyss-bloom filter (e.g. genome_k25_m100M_l1.bloom, k=25) and need exons, IEBs and a splice graph in GFA1 without a reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-intron-exon-boundary-detection))
- **kmer-based-iteration-termination-evaluation**: Use when running HECIL's iterative learning component on long-read/short-read hybrid correction and you need a data-driven stopping criterion and per-iteration quality metric based on unique k-mer counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-iteration-termination-evaluation))
- **kmer-based-metagenome-distance-computation**: Use when you have a set of FASTA/FASTQ metagenome samples (e.g. HMP 16S rRNA amplicons, HMP WGS reads, CAMI PacBio toy reads) and need an all-vs-all distance matrix to cluster samples by community composition and abundance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-metagenome-distance-computation))
- **kmer-based-qv-estimation**: Reference for assessing assembly consensus accuracy (QV) when no trusted reference exists but high-coverage short reads (≥60X recommended) and a HiFi-based assembly are available, or when auditing where residual erroneous k-mers reside. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-qv-estimation))
- **kmer-based-sequence-classification**: Use when metagenomic FASTQ reads must be taxonomically binned against large RefSeq reference sets, especially at strain/assembly level where default ganon2 cutoff and filter settings need threshold sweeping to reach acceptable sensitivity/precision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-sequence-classification))
- **kmer-based-sequence-clustering-interpretation**: Use when two clustering partitions (e.g., Snekmer AAR-kmer clusters and MMseqs2 sequence-similarity clusters) over the same protein set must be compared, and you need shared/non-shared fractions and union coverage (e.g., ~85% combined assignment). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-sequence-clustering-interpretation))
- **kmer-based-sequence-matching**: Use when reads must be quickly matched or filtered against a reference without producing alignments — e.g. removing WGS reads that share no 25-mer with a de novo transcriptome assembly, or pre-screening reads for contamination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-based-sequence-matching))
- **kmer-bray-curtis-dissimilarity-sampling**: Use when you need to explain per-habitat species-level error rates of bespoke (q2-clawback) taxonomic weights by how often similar marker-gene sequences in the same habitat originate from different species, e.g. reproducing the paper's Fig. 5 confusion-index analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/kmer-bray-curtis-dissimilarity-sampling))
- **kmer-canonicalization-and-counting**: Use when preparing training genomes or metagenomic FASTA/FASTQ reads for a k-mer-embedding DNN classifier and you need a canonical k-mer-to-index vocabulary file backing the embedding layer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-canonicalization-and-counting))
- **kmer-composition-feature-extraction**: Use when preparing composition features for contig binning: you have assembled contigs (FASTA, typically &gt;=1000 bp after filtering) and need k-mer frequency vectors, optionally combined across multiple k values, to feed a clustering or binning algorithm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-composition-feature-extraction))
- **kmer-context-binary-classifier-training**: Use when you have Albacore-basecalled R9 nanopore data (fastq + fast5), a reference fasta, eventalign TSVs, and known motif sites (e.g. E. coli GATC) with labels to train methylation classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-context-binary-classifier-training))
- **kmer-context-feature-engineering**: Use when PacBio CCS reads with kinetic tags (fi, ri, fp, rp) must be converted into per-k-mer PW/IPD feature matrices to train classifiers that discriminate modified vs canonical bases in defined sequence contexts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-context-feature-engineering))
- **kmer-context-model-training**: Use when tuning DeepBAM feature extraction for R10.4 nanopore CpG methylation calling and you must decide which k-mer context size balances AUC against feature-extraction and calling run-time cost. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-context-model-training))
- **kmer-context-motif-analysis**: Use when PacBio HiFi/CCS BAMs with kinetic tags (fi, ri, fp, rp) are available and the goal is to test whether a modified base (e.g., dZ vs dA) shifts PW or IPD in a sequence-context-dependent manner across specific motif families (TTZTT vs TTATT, TTZGG vs TTAGG). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-context-motif-analysis))
- **kmer-context-set-intersection-subsampling**: Use when constructing a DeepPlant CHH training set from extract_hc_sites npz feature files and you need per-9-mer context parity between methylation-positive and negative samples before model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-context-set-intersection-subsampling))
- **kmer-context-window-parsing**: Use when per-read event observations from nanopolish eventalign must be grouped into mCaller classification windows (num_variables=6 → 11-mer) and filtered by skip count before or during m6A classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-context-window-parsing))
- **kmer-contig-anchoring-to-long-reads**: Use when assembling a genome from hybrid data: accurate short-read contigs (FASTA from an DBG-assembler) plus low-coverage (10x-20x) erroneous long reads, and a high-contiguity backbone (e.g. N50 583 kbp on yeast) is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-contig-anchoring-to-long-reads))
- **kmer-count-dissimilarity-measure-selection**: Use when comparing whole genomes or metagenomes alignment-free with k-mer counts — e.g. 21 primate genomes at k=14, Markov order 12 — and you must choose among 28 dissimilarity measures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-count-dissimilarity-measure-selection))
- **kmer-count-table-generation**: Use when preparing inputs for decOM in a source-tracking benchmark: shotgun metagenome reads (e.g., BioProjects PRJNA1161616 and PRJNA1092107) must be converted to k-mer count tables because decOM does not accept Kraken2 taxonomic profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-count-table-generation))
- **kmer-counter-installation-and-setup**: Use when compiling Fast-SG from source (git clone https://github.com/adigenova/fastsg) and the build requires the KMC k-mer counter binaries placed in $Fast-SG/KMC/bin/ before 'make all' and 'make test' can succeed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-counter-installation-and-setup))
- **kmer-counting-and-multiplicity-filtering**: Use when preprocessing long-read sequencing reads (FASTQ/FASTA) for embedding-based overlap detection: noisy k-mers must be removed before computing IDF-weighted sparse vectors for ANN search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-counting-and-multiplicity-filtering))
- **kmer-counting-database-construction**: Use when building a Kraken classification database from a genome library: you need each distinct k-mer (default k=31) mapped to the LCA taxon of all genomes containing it, using Jellyfish counts plus NCBI taxonomy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-counting-database-construction))
- **kmer-coverage-depth-estimation**: Use when reproducing MetaPlatanus' coverage-depth-based exclusion of interspecies misjoinings or its contig edge-correction, e.g. when contig links or terminal regions must be judged against per-contig k-mer coverage depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-coverage-depth-estimation))
- **kmer-coverage-fraction-quantification**: Use when evaluating whether nanopore/BS-seq derived methylation site lists cover all possible k-mer motifs around a cytosine context (CpG/CHG/CHH), or when reproducing a reported motif-coverage statistic before training a methylation-calling model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-coverage-fraction-quantification))
- **kmer-coverage-verification**: Use when auditing synthetic training sequences for k-mer-based RNA modification models (e.g., EpiNano m6A SVM): check FASTA inputs enumerate every 5-mer and confirm secondary-structure minimization via MFE folding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-coverage-verification))
- **kmer-ctd-hexamer-sequence-encoding**: Use when raw transcript FASTA sequences (lncRNAs and PCTs) must be encoded into sequence-intrinsic numeric features (k-mer, CTD, hexamer scores) to feed a classifier such as CatBoost, especially alongside ORF-derived features from Orfipy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-ctd-hexamer-sequence-encoding))
- **kmer-current-level-comparison**: Use when two homologous direct RNA nanopore datasets (e.g. methylatedU SRR22888950 vs unmethylated SRR22888949 GFP mRNA) have been realigned into signal pileups and you need to quantify per-k-mer current-level shifts attributable to base modifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-current-level-comparison))
- **kmer-dataset-membership-indexing**: Use when you need to ask 'which indexed dataset(s) may contain this sequence?' over thousands of FASTA/FASTQ files and exact per-k-mer counts matter less than scalable, low-footprint membership queries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-dataset-membership-indexing))
- **kmer-db**: Fast memory-efficient k-mer counting and querying for bioinformatics ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/kmer-db/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **kmer-embedding-layer-implementation**: Use when building or reproducing a deep learning taxonomic classifier (e.g. Embed + LSTM + Attention in DeepMicrobes) where short metagenomic reads must be converted from canonical k-mer indices into dense learned embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-embedding-layer-implementation))
- **kmer-embedding-model-training**: Use when metagenomic reads (fastq/fasta) must be classified at species/genus rank by a deep learning model, or when choosing the k-mer length K for a k-mer embedding classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-embedding-model-training))
- **kmer-emission-gaussian-mixture-training**: Use when you need to train a custom nanoHiMe emission model from nanopore event-level alignments of methylated-control DNA (e.g. PCR-amplified E. coli K12 MG1655 treated with M.SssI and/or Hia5) before running mCG or 6mA calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-emission-gaussian-mixture-training))
- **kmer-evolutionary-selection-from-signal**: Use when generating a custom set of salient k-mer detection networks for baseLess from a large read set of a known species, e.g. a 1500 k-mer 16S library, instead of using the pre-generated 16S k-mer models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-evolutionary-selection-from-signal))
- **kmer-exact-match-taxonomic-classification**: Use when classifying large metagenomic read sets (e.g., HiSeq/MiSeq simulated metagenomes, millions of 100 bp Illumina reads) with taxonomic labels, and BLAST-speed alignment is too slow but Megablast-level genus accuracy is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-exact-match-taxonomic-classification))
- **kmer-expected-signal-computation**: Use when reconstructing CHEUI preprocessing that needs expected unmodified ionic-current vectors for 9-mers: converting Nanopolish 5-mer k-mer model means (model_kmer.csv) into length-100 expected signals and distance vectors from eventalign output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-expected-signal-computation))
- **kmer-feature-extraction**: Use when converting long-read FASTQ/FASTA data into a sparse k-mer feature matrix as input to dimensionality reduction and ANN-based overlap detection, replicating Fedrann's FE stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-feature-extraction))
- **kmer-feature-extraction-from-dna-physical-properties**: Use when raw k-mer/sequence-affinity data (e.g. HT-SELEX cycle files, PBM intensity tables) must be converted into physicochemical feature vectors before training an ML affinity regressor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kmer-feature-extraction-from-dna-physical-properties))
- **kmer-feature-weighting-comparison**: Use when benchmarking a k-mer-based sequence similarity pipeline and you must decide which feature weighting (TF, IDF, TF-IDF, raw) and distance metric (cosine vs Euclidean) to use for overlap detection or ANN search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-feature-weighting-comparison))
- **kmer-fingerprint-haplotype-assignment**: Use when a phased long-read assembly pipeline has partitioned most reads into haplotype blocks but leftover unphased reads (in a long-read BAM) must still be assigned to the correct haplotype. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-fingerprint-haplotype-assignment))
- **kmer-frequency-calculation**: Use when input is a set of viral scaffolds (FASTA, ≥2000 bp) destined for binning and you need nucleotide-composition feature vectors (k-mer frequencies, CU, RTF, TUD, ZOM, GC) to compare scaffolds pairwise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-frequency-calculation))
- **kmer-frequency-computation**: Use when you have a set of labeled genomic regions (e.g. HOT vs COLD from ChIP-seq analysis) and need sequence-derived features — k-mer frequencies, GC content, CpG O/E ratio — for a classifier or feature-importance analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kmer-frequency-computation))
- **kmer-frequency-counting**: Use when you need k-mer frequency profiles from raw Illumina reads or genome assemblies — e.g. GenomeScope genome-size fitting, filtering repetitive fragments by 25-mer copy number, or comparing 6-mer composition between assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-frequency-counting))
- **kmer-frequency-counting**: Use when input is FASTA genomic sequences (.fasta/.fa/.fna) or unassembled NGS shotgun reads, and alignment-free pairwise distances (e.g. Ma, D2star, CVtree, Jaccard) must be computed from k-mer count profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-frequency-counting))
- **kmer-frequency-encoding-of-dna-sequences**: Use when raw DNA sequences in a single FASTA file must be converted to numeric feature vectors for alignment-free unsupervised clustering, e.g., as the encoding step before training iDeLUCS networks on genomic signatures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-frequency-encoding-of-dna-sequences))
- **kmer-frequency-seed-selection**: Use when trimming adapters from single-end Illumina-style FASTQ data and no adapter sequence is provided as input, or when you must reconstruct the unknown adapter contaminating reads (e.g. detecting TruSeq adapters like AGATCGGAAGAGCACACGTCTGAACTCCAGTCA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/kmer-frequency-seed-selection))
- **kmer-genome-signature-analysis**: Use when you have a metagenomic or plasmidome assembly (FASTA contigs) and need to separate plasmid from chromosomal sequences and obtain initial phylum-level taxonomic labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-genome-signature-analysis))
- **kmer-genome-signature-plasmid-classification**: Use when you have a metagenomic or isolate SPAdes assembly (contigs/scaffolds &gt;1 kb) and need to bin sequences as plasmid vs chromosome, e.g. reproducing plasmid identification in a genome with known plasmids of 4-60 kb. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-genome-signature-plasmid-classification))
- **kmer-genomic-signature-clustering**: Use when clustering unaligned DNA FASTA sequences into expected clusters without taxonomic identifiers, e.g. mitochondrial (Vertebrata, Protista, Insecta, Fungi), Bacterial, Archaeal, or viral genomes, and comparing against classical and DNA-specialized baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-genomic-signature-clustering))
- **kmer-hash-based-sequence-clustering**: Use when you need to reduce redundancy of very large protein sequence sets (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-hash-based-sequence-clustering))
- **kmer-histogram-analysis**: Use when you need to size a Bloom filter for k-mer-based assembly finishing (e.g., ntEdit+Sealer) from short-read FASTQ data, by measuring the k-mer distribution with ntCard histograms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-histogram-analysis))
- **kmer-index-construction**: Use when a collection of many sequence datasets (hundreds to hundreds of thousands of FASTA/MultiLine FASTA/FASTQ, gzipped or not) needs an approximate membership query index so later queries can list which datasets share k-mers with a query sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-index-construction))
- **kmer-informativeness-selection**: Use when raw scRNA-seq reads have been k-merized (e.g. jellyfish) into a sparse k-mer matrix and you need reference-free, alignment-free cell features: filter uninformative k-mers, then simhash their abundance vectors into fingerprints to form CKGs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-informativeness-selection))
- **kmer-inverted-index-construction**: Use when raw metagenomic FASTA/FASTQ reads must be converted into a k-mer-indexed representation (e.g., k=20) as the input index for Libra's all-vs-all sample distance computation at scale. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-inverted-index-construction))
- **kmer-lca-taxonomy-assignment**: Use when metagenomic reads (e.g. Illumina FASTQ) need fast taxonomic classification via exact k-mer matches and LCA of the taxa sharing those k-mers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-lca-taxonomy-assignment))
- **kmer-matching-threshold-tuning**: Use when running DBG2OLC overlap/layout on PacBio (3GS) long reads with preassembled Illumina contigs and the initial parameter set yields a low backbone N50 or poor anchoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-matching-threshold-tuning))
- **kmer-matrix-generation-from-reads**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq reads (BAM/FASTA) are available and cell-type features must be derived without aligning to a reference genome, e.g. when the reference genome is incomplete or insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-matrix-generation-from-reads))
- **kmer-minimizer-counting**: Use when one or more metagenome-assembled genome FASTA bins (or a multi-fasta of MAGs) need fast, alignment-free taxonomic assignment at strain, species, or genus level with a measurable reliability score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-minimizer-counting))
- **kmer-minimizer-selection**: Use when building or reimplementing a k-mer/LCA lookup database that must support fast per-k-mer queries — e.g. reconstructing Kraken's database where k-mers sharing a 15-bp minimizer are stored consecutively and searched via index lookup plus binary search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-minimizer-selection))
- **kmer-minimizer-taxonomic-classification**: Use when classifying metagenome-assembled genomes (MAGs) or genome FASTAs up to strain/species/genus level without alignment, especially when marker-gene tools like GTDBtk or CAMITAX are too slow or cannot provide strain-level labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-minimizer-taxonomic-classification))
- **kmer-model-signal-conversion**: Use when performing raw-signal (squiggle) alignment for selective sequencing: you have a FASTA reference (e.g., SARS-CoV-2 MN908947.3), an R9.4 pore chemistry k-mer model, and raw reads in BLOW5 format to align in the signal domain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-model-signal-conversion))
- **kmer-motif-synergy-assessment**: Use when after computing deviations for both motif and kmer annotations on the same chromVAR dataset, when you need to determine whether kmers and motifs are redundant predictors of chromatin accessibility variability or provide complementary information for downstream clustering, annotation, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/kmer-motif-synergy-assessment))
- **kmer-nucleotide-frequency-encoding**: Use when encoding short RNA sequence segments (e.g., 41 bp m5C site flanks from A. thaliana or M. musculus benchmark data) into fixed-length numeric features for machine learning, specifically when building the Staem5 feature fusion input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-nucleotide-frequency-encoding))
- **kmer-occurrence-simulation**: Decision support for choosing k-mer lengths for baseLess salient k-mer selection: given target sequence lengths (e.g. 0.6, 1.5, 30 kb) and non-target sequence lengths (30/50 kb), simulate which k values minimize spurious k-mer hits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-occurrence-simulation))
- **kmer-overrepresentation-statistics**: Use when de novo methylation motifs must be extracted from Oxford Nanopore reads by testing k-mer over-representation against a reference genome background with chi-squared statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-overrepresentation-statistics))
- **kmer-parameter-configuration**: Use when preparing to run the MetaCortex two-stage assembly and you need a binary that supports specific k-mer values (e.g. MAXK of 31, 63, 95, 127, 160 or 192) beyond the fixed bioconda build, or when reproducing an assembly with the same k-mer settings as a prior run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-parameter-configuration))
- **kmer-parameter-sweep-analysis**: Use when tuning a k-mer-dependent pipeline (e.g., EXFI's build_baited_bloom_filter/build_splice_graph) and you must justify a fixed k by showing F1 stability across odd k values on a reference-annotated benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-parameter-sweep-analysis))
- **kmer-pattern-position-annotation**: Use when you have mode-wise random-forest-selected position-specific k-mer features from multiple cross-validation partitions and must find the intersection and annotate surviving patterns by position and descriptor mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-pattern-position-annotation))
- **kmer-recursive-spaced-seed-hashing**: Use when long-read FASTA/FASTQ and a draft assembly need lightweight, alignment-free read-to-contig mappings — e.g., to infer contig order/orientation for scaffolding or to detect misassemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-recursive-spaced-seed-hashing))
- **kmer-reference-hash-table-construction**: Use when implementing or reproducing MEM-based repeat discovery (e.g. pal-MEM/E-MEM style) and you need an encoded k-mer-to-reference-position index to seed maximal exact matches against metagenomic reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-reference-hash-table-construction))
- **kmer-rolling-hash-indexing**: Use when constructing a scaffolding graph or read-placement map alignment-free from short (Illumina mate-pair) or long reads against contig sequences, without a full read aligner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-rolling-hash-indexing))
- **kmer-scored-consensus-error-correction**: Use when polishing a high-quality (e.g., QV &gt; 50) HiFi/hifiasm assembly that still carries consensus errors, especially in repeats or gap-filled ONT regions, and overcorrection or switch errors must be avoided. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-scored-consensus-error-correction))
- **kmer-search-index-construction**: Use when rRNA reads (FASTA/FASTQ, amplicon or shotgun) need taxonomy or OTU assignment against a full-length rRNA reference, or when a custom reference database must be indexed for MAPseq classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/kmer-search-index-construction))
- **kmer-seed-graph-construction**: Use when coarse seeded candidate regions from a long-read mapper must be converted into precise alignment anchors, especially for noisy Oxford Nanopore MinION or PacBio reads with repetitive reference loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-seed-graph-construction))
- **kmer-sequence-context-discrepancy-analysis**: Use when comparing per-site methylation frequencies from nanopore tools against a orthogonal reference (e.g. WGBS) and you need to test whether flanking sequence context explains discrepancies at CpG sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-sequence-context-discrepancy-analysis))
- **kmer-sequence-feature-construction**: Use when you have re-squiggled single-read FAST5 files (Tombo, corrected_group RawGenomeCorrected_000) and need per-site 13-mer sequence+signal features to train or run DeepSignal-plant 5mC detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-sequence-feature-construction))
- **kmer-sequence-one-hot-embedding**: Use when preparing segmented k-mer sequence inputs for a transformer-based signal simulator, e.g., feeding uncalled4 eventalign 9-mer chunks into the seq2squiggle FFT encoder-decoder pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-sequence-one-hot-embedding))
- **kmer-sharing-count-interpretation**: Use when running PAC queries against an index of thousands of datasets and you need to turn the raw per-line kmer-sharing counts (e.g. '29719 50 701') into a ranked list of datasets that may contain the query sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-sharing-count-interpretation))
- **kmer-signal-detection-network-design**: Use when you have raw MinION fast5 reads, a reference genome, and a k-mer list, and need to generate custom k-mer detection networks for a sequence absent from baseLess's pre-generated 16S set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-signal-detection-network-design))
- **kmer-signal-hash-table-indexing**: Use when ONT multi-fast5 reads have been Tombo-resquiggled and you must aggregate raw signal events per reference k-mer (default odd k=15) to find modified motifs against a WGA control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-signal-hash-table-indexing))
- **kmer-signal-statistics-analysis**: Use when you have native and control (e.g., WGA) nanopore fast5 reads plus a reference genome and need de novo detection of methylated k-mers/motifs from raw signal statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-signal-statistics-analysis))
- **kmer-size-ablation-comparison**: Use when you need to decide which k-mer size (e.g., k=21 vs k=16) for CKG feature generation gives equal or better cell type classification accuracy across datasets like Chuang, Karaayvaz, PBMC3k, and Lee. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-size-ablation-comparison))
- **kmer-size-hyperparameter-sweep**: Use when configuring a kinetic-signal k-mer classifier (e.g. Z-Calling full-substitution model) and you must justify which k-mer size trades model strength against compute cost before freezing a production artifact. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-size-hyperparameter-sweep))
- **kmer-size-optimization-for-genome-finishing**: Use when preparing to run ntEdit+Sealer on a draft long-read assembly with short reads and you need to select optimal k-mer lengths and Bloom filter size (b) from k-mer distribution histograms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-size-optimization-for-genome-finishing))
- **kmer-stratified-sample-balancing**: Use when constructing a deep-learning training set from Nanopore signal features (e.g. fast5s.C.features.tsv) where methylated/unmethylated cytosines are heavily skewed across k-mer contexts (CpG, CHG, CHH) and the classifier must not confound methylation with sequence context. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/kmer-stratified-sample-balancing))
- **kmer-svm-classifier-training**: Use when benchmarking sequence-based classifiers on binary CRE-vs-background tasks (e.g., mouse E8.25 distal CREs) and a k-mer kernel SVM baseline such as LS-GKM is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kmer-svm-classifier-training))
- **kmer-taxonomic-classifier-execution**: Use when classifying 10k-read HiSeq or MiSeq mock community FASTQ datasets with Kraken (k=31) and Kaiju Greedy-5 to reproduce genus- and phylum-level sensitivity/precision benchmarks, or when deciding between k-mer and protein-level classifiers for short Illumina reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-taxonomic-classifier-execution))
- **kmer-threshold-parameterization**: Use when building or benchmarking approximate sequence search pre-filters over many bins of nucleotide sequences, and you must choose k (e.g. k=19), minimizer/threshold settings, and evaluate sensitivity (false positives) against known bin memberships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-threshold-parameterization))
- **kmer-to-ckg-feature-aggregation**: Use when you already have a per-cell k-mer abundance matrix (from jellyfish counts) and a CKG look-up table mapping k-mer ids to compressed k-mer groups, and need CKG-level cell features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-to-ckg-feature-aggregation))
- **kmer-to-smer-decomposition**: Use when implementing or testing approximate membership queries with abundances over k-mers and you need to reduce false positive calls by indexing s-mers instead of full k-mers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-to-smer-decomposition))
- **kmer-undersampling-for-affinity-training-sets**: Use when preparing uPBM training data for DNAffinity's random-forest regressor: 35-mer affinities are dominated by weak binders and the PWM-defined high-affinity motif must be emphasized (10x weight) after MEME-based trimming. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kmer-undersampling-for-affinity-training-sets))
- **kmer-uniqueness-analysis-across-binnings**: Use when evaluating or reproducing Raptor's minimizer-based representative k-mer statistics (Table 8), e.g. to check how quickly r(k) collapses toward 1.00 as k grows for a given number of bins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmer-uniqueness-analysis-across-binnings))
- **kmer-variability-deviation-analysis**: Use when scATAC-seq/DNase counts in peaks vary between cells or samples and de novo motif discovery is the goal: rank bias-corrected k-mer deviations by covariance, assemble PWMs with assembleKmers, then match to JASPAR via pwmDistance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/kmer-variability-deviation-analysis))
- **kmer-vector-distance-computation**: Use when protein sequences have been recoded into AAR-kmer vectors and you need a pairwise correlation/distance matrix, thresholded into a graph, to test whether protein families form unconnected subnetworks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/kmer-vector-distance-computation))
- **kmerseek-interrupted-index**: Resume a kmerseek v0.4.0 index/search after a Slurm memory kill, especially when CURRENT exists but calibration failed. ([seanome/2024-kmerseek-analysis](https://github.com/seanome/2024-kmerseek-analysis/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/staging/kmerseek-interrupted-index) · ★1)
- **kmerseek-sherlock-source-build**: Use when building kmerseek from source on Sherlock, particularly when bindgen cannot load libclang or PyO3 selects system Python 3.6. ([seanome/2024-kmerseek-analysis](https://github.com/seanome/2024-kmerseek-analysis/tree/HEAD/.Codex/skills/staging/kmerseek-sherlock-source-build) · ★1)
- **kmert-accessibility-feature-selection**: Use when the input is a raw scATAC-seq k-mer or peak count matrix (e.g. 2034 QC-passed human bone-marrow cells) and the goal is trajectory inference or regulator discovery without gene-level annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmert-accessibility-feature-selection))
- **kmert-to-transcription-factor-mapping**: Use when a scATAC-seq trajectory has been reconstructed with STREAM using k-mer features and you need to identify which transcription factors drive each detected branch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kmert-to-transcription-factor-mapping))
- **kmf-official-app-field-research**: Reuse this workflow to answer a narrow KMF protocol question by tracing how the official Junce Home Android app talks to the meter, how it decodes a target field, and how that behavior should be implemented in the native app in this repository. ([mohammedi-haroune/kmf-ble-android](https://github.com/mohammedi-haroune/kmf-ble-android/tree/HEAD/planning/resources/skills/kmf-official-app-field-research))
- **kmg-adr-guide**: Auto-surface ADR creation when user makes architectural decisions or chooses between technical approaches ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-adr-guide) · ★6)
- **kmg-auto-recall**: Auto-invoke knowledge graph search when user asks about project history, past decisions, or previously solved problems ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-auto-recall) · ★6)
- **kmg-brainstorm-recall**: Ensure the knowledge graph is consulted before any recommendation is made ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-brainstorm-recall) · ★6)
- **kmg-capture-router**: Route capture-that / remember-that requests to the correct destination via auto-detection ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-capture-router) · ★6)
- **kmg-doc-update-router**: Intercept explicit doc-update requests and route to the correct command ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-doc-update-router) · ★6)
- **kmg-docs-impact-scan**: Fires on pre-ship signals to scan for docs affected by code changes ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-docs-impact-scan) · ★6)
- **kmg-execute-plan**: Enforce zero-deviation plan execution when user invokes plan implementation ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-execute-plan) · ★6)
- **kmg-knowledge-graph-usage**: Provide orientation to the Knowledge Graph system architecture and guidance for knowledge capture ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-knowledge-graph-usage) · ★6)
- **kmg-lesson-capture**: Auto-capture lessons when user solves complex bugs, makes breakthroughs, or completes debugging sessions ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-lesson-capture) · ★6)
- **kmg-paperwork-audit**: Fires on pre-ship signals to check issue/enhancement status accuracy and session-summary currency before push ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-paperwork-audit) · ★6)
- **kmg-plan-gate**: Enforce user approval gates after superpowers planning and execution skills complete ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-plan-gate) · ★6)
- **kmg-rules-capture**: Detect implicit mid-session behavioral corrections and route to the correct rules file ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-rules-capture) · ★6)
- **kmg-session-wrap**: Prompt for session summary when user indicates they are stopping work or approaching context limits ([technomensch/knowledge-graph](https://github.com/technomensch/knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/kmg-session-wrap) · ★6)
